        border-radius: 999px;\n
        box-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);\n
        pointer-events: none;\n
        user-select: none;\n
    }\n
</style>\n

<script>\n
(function () {\n
    var badges = {};\n
    var badge = document.querySelector('.page-marker');\n
    var baseUrl = badge ? badge.getAttribute('data-base-url') : '';\n
\n
    if (!baseUrl) { return; }\n
\n
    function place(modal, number) {\n
        var dialog = modal && modal.querySelector('.modal-dialog');\n
        if (!dialog || !number || dialog.querySelector('.page-marker-modal')) { return; }\n
\n
        var el = document.createElement('div');\n
        el.className = 'page-marker-modal';\n
        el.textContent = number;\n
        dialog.appendChild(el);\n
    }\n
\n
    function label(modal) {\n
        var id = modal && modal.getAttribute('id');\n
        if (!id) { return; }\n
\n
        if (id in badges) {\n
            place(modal, badges[id]);\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        fetch(baseUrl + '?key=' + encodeURIComponent(id), { headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' } })\n
            .then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null; })\n
            .then(function (data) {\n
                if (data && data.number) {\n
                    badges[id] = data.number;\n
                    place(modal, data.number);\n
                }\n
            })\n
            .catch(function () {});\n
    }\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('shown.bs.modal', function (e) {\n
        label(e.target);\n
    });\n
})();\n
</script>\n

                    <style>\n
    /* =====================================================================\n
       Connectivity signal ΓÇö scoped styles.\n
       Namespaced under .cs-root / .cs-*, so it never leaks into the page.\n
       Per-instance sizing is carried by the CSS custom properties set inline\n
       on each rendered wrapper.\n
       ===================================================================== */\n
\n
    .cs-root {\n
        /* Palette ΓÇö matches the working vanilla-JS prototype (Bootstrap-style\n
           success/danger, no neon/pastel). */\n
        --cs-green: #198754;\n
        --cs-green-glow: rgba(25, 135, 84, 0.55);\n
        --cs-red: #dc3545;\n
        --cs-red-glow: rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.55);\n
\n
        /* Fallbacks ΓÇö normally overridden by the inline style attribute. */\n
        --cs-dot-size: 16px;\n
        --cs-stage: 64px;\n
\n
        position: relative;\n
        display: inline-flex;\n
        align-items: center;\n
        justify-content: center;\n
        width: var(--cs-stage);\n
        height: var(--cs-stage);\n
        flex-shrink: 0;\n
        user-select: none;\n
    }\n
\n
    .cs-ring,\n
    .cs-dot {\n
        box-sizing: border-box;\n
    }\n
\n
    /* --- The dot ----------------------------------------------------------\n
       The base style IS STATE 1 (stable): green, steady, soft glow, completely\n
       static. A no-Alpine cold load therefore renders a correct resting state\n
       with no animation classes applied. */\n
    .cs-dot {\n
        width: var(--cs-dot-size);\n
        height: var(--cs-dot-size);\n
        border-radius: 9999px;\n
        background-color: var(--cs-green);\n
        box-shadow: 0 0 var(--cs-glow-blur) var(--cs-glow-spread) var(--cs-green-glow);\n
    }\n
\n
    /* STATE 2 ΓÇö offline: red + the fade-struggle breathing loop (opacity AND\n
       glow intensity, ~1.8s). Fading never happens in any green state. */\n
    .cs-dot--offline {\n
        background-color: var(--cs-red);\n
        animation: cs-fade-struggle 1.8s ease-in-out infinite;\n
    }\n
\n
    /* --- Rings ------------------------------------------------------------\n
       Always in the DOM but sitting at opacity 0 until the root gains\n
       .is-rippling, so they stay hidden on a no-Alpine cold load without\n
       depending on x-show/x-cloak. */\n
    .cs-rings {\n
        position: absolute;\n
        inset: 0;\n
        --cs-ring-color: var(--cs-green);\n
        pointer-events: none;\n
    }\n
\n
    .cs-rings--red   { --cs-ring-color: var(--cs-red); }\n
    .cs-rings--green { --cs-ring-color: var(--cs-green); }\n
\n
    .cs-ring {\n
        position: absolute;\n
        left: 50%;\n
        top: 50%;\n
        width: var(--cs-dot-size);\n
        height: var(--cs-dot-size);\n
        margin-left: calc(var(--cs-dot-size) / -2);\n
        margin-top: calc(var(--cs-dot-size) / -2);\n
        border: calc(var(--cs-dot-size) / 6) solid var(--cs-ring-color);\n
        border-radius: 9999px;\n
        opacity: 0;\n
        pointer-events: none;\n
        will-change: transform, opacity;\n
        animation-name: none;\n
        animation-duration: 1.8s;\n
        animation-timing-function: cubic-bezier(0.22, 0.61, 0.36, 1);\n
        animation-delay: 0s;\n
        animation-iteration-count: infinite;\n
    }\n
\n
    /* Three rings, staggered ~0.5s apart (negative delays start them all\n
       mid-cycle so the stagger is visible from the very first frame). */\n
    .cs-ring--2 { animation-delay: -0.5s; }\n
    .cs-ring--3 { animation-delay: -1s; }\n
\n
    .cs-root.is-rippling .cs-ring {\n
        animation-name: cs-ripple;\n
    }\n
\n
    /* Ripple: scale 1 -> 4.5 while fading opacity 0.65 -> 0. Used by rings. */\n
    @keyframes cs-ripple {\n
        0%   { transform: scale(1);   opacity: 0.65; }\n
        100% { transform: scale(4.5); opacity: 0; }\n
    }\n
\n
    /* Fade-struggle: breathing opacity AND glow between 1.0 and ~0.3.\n
       Used ONLY by the red/offline dot (STATE 2). */\n
    @keyframes cs-fade-struggle {\n
        0%, 100% {\n
            opacity: 1;\n
            box-shadow: 0 0 var(--cs-glow-blur) var(--cs-glow-spread) var(--cs-red-glow);\n
        }\n
        50% {\n
            opacity: 0.3;\n
            box-shadow: 0 0 2px 0 rgba(220, 53, 69, 0.25);\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    /* Screen-reader-only live label (updates via x-text). */\n
    .cs-sr-only {\n
        position: absolute;\n
        width: 1px;\n
        height: 1px;\n
        margin: -1px;\n
        padding: 0;\n
        border: 0;\n
        clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);\n
        overflow: hidden;\n
        white-space: nowrap;\n
    }\n
\n
    /* Respect reduced-motion: no pulsing, no ripple ΓÇö colour alone still\n
       communicates the state. */\n
    @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {\n
        .cs-ring { display: none; }\n
        .cs-dot--offline {\n
            animation: none;\n
            box-shadow: 0 0 var(--cs-glow-blur) var(--cs-glow-spread) var(--cs-red-glow);\n
        }\n
    }\n
</style>\n

<script>\n
    (function () {\n
        'use strict';\n
\n
        /* STATE 3 -> STATE 1 settle window, in milliseconds. */\n
        var SETTLE_MS = 5000;\n
\n
        /* Factory returning the Alpine component data object. Defined as a\n
           plain function so the component works no matter whether Alpine has\n
           booted before or after this classic script runs. */\n
        function mawaConnectivitySignal(labels) {\n
            labels = labels || {};\n
\n
            return {\n
                /* 'stable' | 'offline' | 'reconnecting' */\n
                state: 'stable',\n
                isRippling: false,   // toggles the ripple rings\n
                settleTimer: null,   // pending STATE 3 -> STATE 1 timeout id\n
                statusLabel: labels.online || 'Online',\n
                labels: labels,\n
                _bound: false,       // guards against double binding\n
\n
                /* Runs when Alpine boots the component. Listener wiring happens\n
                   here (plain addEventListener, as required) so the cancel-\n
                   stale-timer logic is co-located with the state changes. */\n
                init: function () {\n
                    var self = this;\n
\n
                    // Idempotency guard: Alpine auto-invokes init() AND x-init\n
                    // may call it again ΓÇö never bind the same listeners twice.\n
                    if (this._bound) { return; }\n
                    this._bound = true;\n
\n
                    // Cold start reflects the real browser state rather than\n
                    // always defaulting to the resting state.\n
                    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.onLine === false) {\n
                        this.gotoOffline();\n
                    } else {\n
                        this.gotoStable();\n
                    }\n
\n
                    // Closure-bound handlers keep `this` pointing at the\n
                    // component no matter how the event fires.\n
                    this.boundOnline = function () {\n
                        self.gotoReconnecting();\n
                    };\n
\n
                    this.boundOffline = function () {\n
                        // Spec: a running State-3 "settle to stable" timer must\n
                        // be cancelled ΓÇö a stale 5s timer must never flip us to\n
                        // stable while we are genuinely offline again.\n
                        if (self.settleTimer) {\n
                            window.clearTimeout(self.settleTimer);\n
                            self.settleTimer = null;\n
                        }\n
                        self.gotoOffline();\n
                    };\n
\n
                    window.addEventListener('online', this.boundOnline);\n
                    window.addEventListener('offline', this.boundOffline);\n
                },\n
\n
                /* Alpine teardown hook (SPA node removal): clear the timer and\n
                   listeners so nothing fires against a detached component. */\n
                destroy: function () {\n
                    if (this.settleTimer) {\n
                        window.clearTimeout(this.settleTimer);\n
                        this.settleTimer = null;\n
                    }\n
                    if (this.boundOnline) { window.removeEventListener('online', this.boundOnline); }\n
                    if (this.boundOffline) { window.removeEventListener('offline', this.boundOffline); }\n
                    this.boundOnline = null;\n
                    this.boundOffline = null;\n
                    this._bound = false;\n
                },\n
\n
                /* STATE 1 ΓÇö stable / online (resting). */\n
                gotoStable: function () {\n
                    this.state = 'stable';\n
                    this.isRippling = false;\n
                    this.statusLabel = this.labels.online || 'Online';\n
                },\n
\n
                /* STATE 2 ΓÇö offline: red dot + red rings, indefinite. */\n
                gotoOffline: function () {\n
                    this.state = 'offline';\n
                    this.isRippling = true;\n
                    this.statusLabel = this.labels.offline || 'Offline';\n
                },\n
\n
                /* STATE 3 ΓÇö reconnecting: green, solid (no fading), green rings\n
                   ripple for exactly SETTLE_MS, then settle to STATE 1. */\n
                gotoReconnecting: function () {\n
                    var self = this;\n
\n
                    // Restart the settle window if online fires again mid-settle.\n
                    if (this.settleTimer) {\n
                        window.clearTimeout(this.settleTimer);\n
                    }\n
\n
                    this.state = 'reconnecting';\n
                    this.isRippling = true;\n
                    this.statusLabel = this.labels.reconnecting || 'Reconnecting';\n
\n
                    this.settleTimer = window.setTimeout(function () {\n
                        self.settleTimer = null;\n
                        self.gotoStable();\n
                    }, SETTLE_MS);\n
                }\n
            };\n
        }\n
\n
        /* Alpine evaluates `x-data` expressions against the window scope, so\n
           exposing the factory globally lets the component bootstrap even when\n
           Alpine boots after this classic script (deferred Vite build, CDN). */\n
        window.mawaConnectivitySignal = mawaConnectivitySignal;\n
\n
        // If Alpine is already loaded (non-deferred CDN), also register the\n
        // idiomatic Alpine.data variant ΓÇö behaviour is identical either way.\n
        if (window.Alpine && typeof window.Alpine.data === 'function') {\n
            window.Alpine.data('mawaConnectivitySignal', function () {\n
                return mawaConnectivitySignal();\n
            });\n
        }\n
    })();\n
</script>\n
\n

    x-init="init()"\n
    class="cs-root relative inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center cs-avatar-corner"\n

<script>\n
(function () {\n
    var root = document.documentElement;\n
    var btn  = document.getElementById('monetixDarkToggle');\n
    var icon = document.getElementById('monetixDarkIcon');\n
    if (!btn || !icon) { return; }\n
\n
    function isDark() {\n
        return root.classList.contains('monetix-dark') ||\n
               root.getAttribute('data-bs-theme') === 'dark';\n
    }\n
    function apply(enabled) {\n
        root.classList.toggle('monetix-dark', enabled);\n
        root.setAttribute('data-bs-theme', enabled ? 'dark' : 'light');\n
        icon.className = enabled ? 'bi bi-sun-fill' : 'bi bi-moon-stars-fill';\n
        root.dataset.userTheme = enabled ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
    }\n
    function persist(enabled) {\n
        fetch('http://localhost/account/preferences/theme', {\n
            method: 'POST',\n
            headers: {\n
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': '6hTQljqlObsr7IdUqMRvsnhbRMoLPSz7xChEFkN6'\n
            },\n
            body: JSON.stringify({ theme: enabled ? 'dark' : 'light' })\n
        });\n
    }\n
    var saved = null;\n
    try { saved = localStorage.getItem('monetix_ui_dark_admin'); } catch (e) {}\n
    var serverTheme = root.dataset.userTheme || 'default';\n
    if (saved !== null && serverTheme === 'default') {\n
        apply(saved === '1');\n
        persist(saved === '1');\n
    } else if (serverTheme !== 'default') {\n
        apply(serverTheme === 'dark');\n
        icon.className = isDark() ? 'bi bi-sun-fill' : 'bi bi-moon-stars-fill';\n
    } else {\n
        icon.className = isDark() ? 'bi bi-sun-fill' : 'bi bi-moon-stars-fill';\n
    }\n
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
        var enabled = !isDark();\n
        apply(enabled);\n
        persist(enabled);\n
    });\n
})();\n
\n
(function () {\n
    var list = document.getElementById('monetixNotifList');\n
    if (!list) { return; }\n
\n
    var dot = document.getElementById('monetixNotifDot');\n
    var readAllBtn = document.getElementById('monetixNotifReadAll');\n
    var readAllUrl = readAllBtn ? readAllBtn.getAttribute('data-read-all-url') : '';\n
    var token = '6hTQljqlObsr7IdUqMRvsnhbRMoLPSz7xChEFkN6';\n
\n
    function post(url, body) {\n
        return fetch(url, {\n
            method: 'POST',\n
            headers: {\n
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n
                'Accept': 'application/json',\n
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': token\n
            },\n
            body: body ? JSON.stringify(body) : '{}'\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    function decrementDot(count) {\n
        if (!dot) { return; }\n
        var current = parseInt(dot.textContent, 10) || 0;\n
        var next = Math.max(0, current - count);\n
        if (next <= 0) {\n
            dot.classList.remove('show');\n
        } else {\n
            dot.textContent = next > 99 ? '99+' : next;\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    list.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        var item = e.target.closest('.notification-item');\n
        if (!item) { return; }\n
\n
        var markReadUrl = item.getAttribute('data-mark-read-url');\n
        var wasUnread = item.classList.contains('unread');\n
\n
        if (wasUnread) {\n
            item.classList.remove('unread');\n
            item.classList.add('read');\n
            decrementDot(1);\n
        }\n
\n
        if (markReadUrl && wasUnread) {\n
            e.preventDefault();\n
            post(markReadUrl).finally(function () {\n
                window.location.href = item.getAttribute('href');\n
            });\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    function hideDot() {\n
        if (!dot) { return; }\n
        dot.textContent = '0';\n
        dot.classList.remove('show');\n
        dot.style.display = 'none';\n
    }\n
\n
    if (readAllBtn) {\n
        readAllBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            var items = list.querySelectorAll('.notification-item.unread');\n
            for (var i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {\n
                items[i].classList.remove('unread');\n
                items[i].classList.add('read');\n
            }\n
            hideDot();\n
            if (readAllUrl) {\n
                post(readAllUrl).then(function (res) {\n
                    if (!res.ok) { throw new Error('mark-all failed'); }\n
                    // success ΓÇö keep dot hidden\n
                }).catch(function () {\n
                    // rollback optimistic UI on failure so user can retry\n
                    for (var j = 0; j < items.length; j++) {\n
                        items[j].classList.remove('read');\n
                        items[j].classList.add('unread');\n
                    }\n
                    if (dot) {\n
                        dot.style.display = '';\n
                        dot.classList.add('show');\n
                    }\n
                });\n
            }\n
        });\n
    }\n
})();\n
\n
(function () {\n
    var btn = document.getElementById('monetixSidebarToggle');\n
    if (!btn) { return; }\n
\n
    var root = document.documentElement;\n
    var backdrop = document.getElementById('monetixSidebarBackdrop');\n
    var COLLAPSE_KEY = 'monetix_sidebar_collapsed';\n
    var mobileQuery = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 768px)');\n
\n
    function isMobile() {\n
        return mobileQuery.matches;\n
    }\n
\n
    function updateBtn() {\n
        var i = btn.querySelector('i');\n
        if (i) {\n
            i.className = isMobile()\n
                ? (root.classList.contains('sidebar-open') ? 'bi bi-x' : 'bi bi-list')\n
                : 'bi bi-list';\n
        }\n
        btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', isMobile()\n
            ? (root.classList.contains('sidebar-open') ? 'true' : 'false')\n
            : (root.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') ? 'false' : 'true'));\n
    }\n
\n
    function syncLayoutMode() {\n
        if (isMobile()) {\n
            // Mobile uses the off-canvas drawer ΓÇö ignore the desktop collapse state.\n
            root.classList.remove('sidebar-collapsed');\n
            closeDrawer();\n
        } else {\n
            var saved = null;\n
            try { saved = localStorage.getItem(COLLAPSE_KEY); } catch (e) {}\n
            if (saved === '1') {\n
                root.classList.add('sidebar-collapsed');\n
            }\n
            updateBtn();\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // Replay the label slide-in only when the drawer is expanded by the user ΓÇö\n
    // never on page load.\n
    function animateLabels() {\n
        var labels = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav-link .sidebar-label');\n
        for (var i = 0; i < labels.length; i++) {\n
            labels[i].classList.remove('sidebar-label-anim');\n
            void labels[i].offsetWidth;\n
            labels[i].classList.add('sidebar-label-anim');\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // Restore the persisted desktop collapse state (skipped on mobile).\n
    syncLayoutMode();\n
\n
    function openDrawer() {\n
        root.classList.add('sidebar-open');\n
        if (backdrop) { backdrop.classList.add('show'); }\n
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n
        animateLabels();\n
        updateBtn();\n
    }\n
\n
    function closeDrawer() {\n
        root.classList.remove('sidebar-open');\n
        if (backdrop) { backdrop.classList.remove('show'); }\n
        document.body.style.overflow = '';\n
        updateBtn();\n
    }\n
\n
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
        if (isMobile()) {\n
            if (root.classList.contains('sidebar-open')) {\n
                closeDrawer();\n
            } else {\n
                openDrawer();\n
            }\n
        } else {\n
            var wasCollapsed = root.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed');\n
            root.classList.toggle('sidebar-collapsed');\n
            try {\n
                localStorage.setItem(COLLAPSE_KEY, root.classList.contains('sidebar-collapsed') ? '1' : '0');\n
            } catch (e) {}\n
            if (wasCollapsed) { animateLabels(); }\n
            updateBtn();\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    if (backdrop) {\n
        backdrop.addEventListener('click', closeDrawer);\n
    }\n
\n
    mobileQuery.addEventListener('change', syncLayoutMode);\n
\n
    // Collapsed-mode avatar is now a dropdown toggle ΓÇö keep the sidebar from\n
    // clipping the open card while the dropdown is showing.\n
    var userCard = document.querySelector('.sidebar-user-card');\n
    if (userCard) {\n
        var sidebar = userCard.closest('.sidebar');\n
        userCard.addEventListener('shown.bs.dropdown', function () {\n
            if (sidebar) { sidebar.classList.add('sidebar-no-clip'); }\n
        });\n
        userCard.addEventListener('hidden.bs.dropdown', function () {\n
            if (sidebar) { sidebar.classList.remove('sidebar-no-clip'); }\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    updateBtn();\n
})();\n
\n
(function() {\n
    // Wait for DOM to be ready\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {\n
\n
        // Step 1: Get all collapsible groups inside the sidebar nav\n
        const navGroups = document.querySelectorAll('.sidebar .nav .nav-group .collapse');\n
        if (!navGroups.length) return;\n
\n
        // Step 2: Function to close all other groups except the one that is being shown\n
        function closeOtherGroups(currentElement) {\n
            const parentNav = currentElement.closest('.nav');\n
            if (!parentNav) return;\n
\n
            // Find all open collapse elements inside the same nav\n
            const openGroups = parentNav.querySelectorAll('.nav-group .collapse.show');\n
            openGroups.forEach(function(other) {\n
                // If it's not the current element, and it's not the active group (hasActive)\n
                if (other !== currentElement) {\n
                    // Check if this other group contains an active link ΓÇö if yes, keep it open\n
                    const hasActive = other.querySelector('.nav-link.active') !== null;\n
                    if (!hasActive) {\n
                        // Use Bootstrap's Collapse API to hide\n
                        const instance = bootstrap.Collapse.getInstance(other);\n
                        if (instance) {\n
                            instance.hide();\n
                        } else {\n
                            // If no instance, just remove class (fallback)\n
                            other.classList.remove('show');\n
                        }\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            });\n
        }\n
\n
        // Step 3: Attach show.bs.collapse event to each group\n
        navGroups.forEach(function(el) {\n
            const targetId = el.id;\n
            if (!targetId) return;\n
\n
            // On show, close other groups and save state\n
            el.addEventListener('show.bs.collapse', function(e) {\n
                // Prevent if this is a programmatic trigger from the active guard\n
                // (we'll handle it manually)\n
                closeOtherGroups(el);\n
                // Save state: this group is now open\n
                localStorage.setItem('sidebar_group_' + targetId, 'true');\n
            });\n
\n
            // On hide, save state (only if not active)\n
            el.addEventListener('hide.bs.collapse', function(e) {\n
                const hasActive = el.querySelector('.nav-link.active') !== null;\n
                if (!hasActive) {\n
                    localStorage.setItem('sidebar_group_' + targetId, 'false');\n
                }\n
            });\n
        });\n
\n
        // Step 4: Restore states on page load\n
        navGroups.forEach(function(el) {\n
            const targetId = el.id;\n
            if (!targetId) return;\n
            const stored = localStorage.getItem('sidebar_group_' + targetId);\n
            const hasActive = el.querySelector('.nav-link.active') !== null;\n
\n
            // If the group has an active link, force it open\n
            if (hasActive) {\n
                el.classList.add('show');\n
                const btn = document.querySelector('[data-bs-target="#' + targetId + '"]');\n
                if (btn) btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');\n
                // Also ensure other groups are closed (but preserve active group)\n
                closeOtherGroups(el);\n
                return;\n
            }\n
\n
            // Otherwise, restore from localStorage\n
            if (stored === 'true') {\n
                el.classList.add('show');\n
                const btn = document.querySelector('[data-bs-target="#' + targetId + '"]');\n
                if (btn) btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');\n
            } else {\n
                el.classList.remove('show');\n
                const btn = document.querySelector('[data-bs-target="#' + targetId + '"]');\n
                if (btn) btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        // Step 5: When any collapse is hidden, ensure we don't accidentally close an active group\n
        // (But the hide event listener already handles this)\n
    });\n
})();\n
\n
(function () {\n
    var loader = document.getElementById('monetixSkeletonLoader');\n
    if (!loader) { return; }\n
\n
    var MIN_TIME = 334;\n
    var MAX_TIME = 5000;\n
    var started = Date.now();\n
    var done = false;\n
\n
    function hide() {\n
        if (done) { return; }\n
        done = true;\n
        loader.classList.add('hide');\n
        setTimeout(function () { loader.remove(); }, 300);\n
    }\n
\n
    function tryHide() {\n
        var elapsed = Date.now() - started;\n
        if (elapsed >= MIN_TIME) {\n
            hide();\n
        } else {\n
            setTimeout(tryHide, MIN_TIME - elapsed);\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // Safety net: never leave the skeleton blocking the page.\n
    setTimeout(hide, MAX_TIME);\n
\n
    if (document.readyState === 'complete') {\n
        tryHide();\n
    } else {\n
        window.addEventListener('load', tryHide);\n
    }\n
})();\n
</script>\n

       transform/will-change create containing blocks for position:fixed modals,\n

    body.modal-open .admin-card, body.modal-open .page-header { transform: none !important; }\n

<script>\n
(function () {\n
    if (!window.Monetix || !Monetix.loadPage) { return; }\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        var switcher = document.querySelector('[data-tab-switch]');\n
        if (!switcher || !switcher.contains(e.target)) { return; }\n
        var link = e.target.closest('a[href]');\n
        if (!link) { return; }\n
        var url = link.getAttribute('href');\n
        if (url && url !== location.href) {\n
            e.preventDefault();\n
            // Tab switches are transient states: replace, don't push (Fix A).\n
            Monetix.loadPage(url, { replace: true });\n
        }\n
    });\n
\n
    window.addEventListener('popstate', function () {\n
        // If the user was browsing pages inside a list (1 ΓåÆ 2 ΓåÆ 5), go back to\n
        // the previous page of that same list instead of leaving the list.\n
        var prev = window.Monetix && Monetix.navHistory\n
            ? Monetix.navHistory.previous(window.location.pathname)\n
            : null;\n
        Monetix.loadPage(prev || (window.location.pathname + window.location.search), { push: false });\n
    });\n
})();\n
</script>\n
<script>\n
(function () {\n
    function syncGroupsActive() {\n
        var settingsLink = document.querySelector('[data-academic-settings-link]');\n
        var groupsLink = document.querySelector('[data-groups-link]');\n
        if (!settingsLink || !groupsLink) { return; }\n
        var hash = window.location.hash;\n
        var search = window.location.search;\n
        var isGroupsHash = hash === '#groups';\n
        var isGroupsQuery = search.indexOf('section=groups') !== -1;\n
        if (isGroupsHash || isGroupsQuery) {\n
            groupsLink.classList.add('active');\n
            settingsLink.classList.remove('active');\n
        }\n
    }\n
    function syncYearsPlacementsActive() {\n
        var yearsLink = document.querySelector('[data-academic-years-link]');\n
        var placementsLink = document.querySelector('[data-placements-link]');\n
        if (!yearsLink || !placementsLink) { return; }\n
        var hash = window.location.hash;\n
        var search = window.location.search;\n
        var isYears = hash === '#academic-years' || search.indexOf('section=academic-years') !== -1;\n
        if (isYears) {\n
            yearsLink.classList.add('active');\n
            placementsLink.classList.remove('active');\n
        }\n
    }\n
    function syncAllAcademicAnchors() { syncGroupsActive(); syncYearsPlacementsActive(); }\n
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', syncAllAcademicAnchors);\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', syncAllAcademicAnchors);\n
    syncAllAcademicAnchors();\n
})();\n
</script>\n

<script>\n
window.MAWA_DATE_ORDER = "dmy";\n
(function () {\n
    function orderOf(disp) {\n
        var o = disp && disp.getAttribute ? disp.getAttribute('data-date-order') : '';\n
        if (o === 'mdy' || o === 'ymd' || o === 'dmy') return o;\n
        var g = window.MAWA_DATE_ORDER;\n
        return (g === 'mdy' || g === 'ymd' || g === 'dmy') ? g : 'dmy';\n
    }\n
    function toDisplay(iso, order) {\n
        var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso || '');\n
        if (!m) return '';\n
        if (order === 'mdy') return m[2] + '/' + m[3] + '/' + m[1];\n
        if (order === 'ymd') return m[1] + '/' + m[2] + '/' + m[3];\n
        return m[3] + '/' + m[2] + '/' + m[1];\n
    }\n
    function toIso(text, order) {\n
        var t = (text || '').trim();\n
        var m;\n
        if (order === 'ymd') {\n
            m = /^(\d{4})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})$/.exec(t);\n
            if (!m) return '';\n
            var y = +m[1], mo = +m[2], d = +m[3];\n
            var dt = new Date(y, mo - 1, d);\n
            if (dt.getFullYear() !== y || dt.getMonth() !== mo - 1 || dt.getDate() !== d) return '';\n
            return m[1] + '-' + String(mo).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d).padStart(2, '0');\n
        }\n
        m = /^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/.exec(t);\n
        if (!m) return '';\n
        var a = +m[1], b = +m[2], yy = +m[3];\n
        var dd = order === 'mdy' ? b : a;\n
        var mm = order === 'mdy' ? a : b;\n
        var chk = new Date(yy, mm - 1, dd);\n
        if (chk.getFullYear() !== yy || chk.getMonth() !== mm - 1 || chk.getDate() !== dd) return '';\n
        return yy + '-' + String(mm).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(dd).padStart(2, '0');\n
    }\n
    // Keep only date characters (digits + slash), capped at maxLen.\n
    function stripToDateChars(value, maxLen) {\n
        var out = '';\n
        for (var i = 0; i < value.length && out.length < maxLen; i++) {\n
            var ch = value.charAt(i);\n
            if ((ch >= '0' && ch <= '9') || ch === '/') out += ch;\n
        }\n
        return out;\n
    }\n
    // Caret position after stripping: kept chars before the old caret,\n
    // clamped to the cleaned length.\n
    function caretAfterStrip(raw, pos, maxPos) {\n
        var kept = 0;\n
        for (var i = 0; i < raw.length && i < pos; i++) {\n
            var ch = raw.charAt(i);\n
            if ((ch >= '0' && ch <= '9') || ch === '/') kept++;\n
        }\n
        return Math.min(kept, maxPos);\n
    }\n
    // Parse free-typed text to ISO (strict ΓÇö rejects overflow like 31/02).\n
    // Accepts tenant order with / - . separators, 8-digit runs (DDMMYYYY /\n
    // MMDDYYYY / YYYYMMDD per order), and year-first ISO in any order.\n
    function parseToIso(text, order) {\n
        var t = (text || '').trim().replace(/[.]/g, '/').replace(/-/g, '/');\n
        var m = /^(\d{4})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})$/.exec(t);\n
        if (m) {\n
            var y0 = +m[1], mo0 = +m[2], d0 = +m[3];\n
            var dt0 = new Date(y0, mo0 - 1, d0);\n
            if (dt0.getFullYear() === y0 && dt0.getMonth() === mo0 - 1 && dt0.getDate() === d0) {\n
                return m[1] + '-' + String(mo0).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(d0).padStart(2, '0');\n
            }\n
            return '';\n
        }\n
        var d8 = /^(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{4})$/.exec(t);\n
        if (order !== 'ymd' && d8) {\n
            var viaDayFirst = toIso(d8[1] + '/' + d8[2] + '/' + d8[3], order);\n
            if (viaDayFirst) return viaDayFirst;\n
            // Day-first split invalid (e.g. stripped ISO "20260911" in dmy):\n
            // fall through to the year-first attempt below.\n
        }\n
        var y8 = /^(\d{4})(\d{2})(\d{2})$/.exec(t);\n
        if (y8) {\n
            return parseToIso(y8[1] + '/' + y8[2] + '/' + y8[3], order);\n
        }\n
        return toIso(t, order);\n
    }\n
    function fullLen(order) { return 10; }\n
    // Set a hidden ISO input and notify listeners (e.g. DOB ΓåÆ age calculators).\n
    // Programmatic .value sets fire no events on their own.\n
    function setHiddenIso(hidden, iso) {\n
        if (!hidden) return;\n
        iso = iso || '';\n
        if ((hidden.value || '') === iso) return;\n
        hidden.value = iso;\n
        try { hidden.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); } catch (e) {}\n
    }\n
    window.tdateSync = function (id) {\n
        var hidden = document.getElementById(id);\n
        var disp = document.getElementById(id + '_display');\n
        if (hidden && disp) disp.value = toDisplay(hidden.value, orderOf(disp));\n
    };\n
    // True when a tenant-date field is submittable (empty or valid ISO present).\n
    window.tdateReady = function (id) {\n
        var hidden = document.getElementById(id);\n
        var disp = document.getElementById(id + '_display');\n
        if (!hidden || !disp) return true;\n
        var t = (disp.value || '').trim();\n
        return t === '' || hidden.value !== '';\n
    };\n
    // Filter-form submit guard: skips submit while any tenant date field is\n
    // incomplete/invalid ΓÇö flagging the first bad field and focusing it so\n
    // the skip is visible instead of silent.\n
    window.guardTdateSubmit = function (el) {\n
        var form = el && el.form ? el.form : (el && el.closest ? el.closest('form') : null);\n
        if (form && form.querySelectorAll) {\n
            var displays = form.querySelectorAll('[data-tdate-display]');\n
            var bad = null;\n
            for (var i = 0; i < displays.length; i++) {\n
                var h = document.getElementById(displays[i].getAttribute('data-tdate-display'));\n
                var t = (displays[i].value || '').trim();\n
                if (t !== '' && (!h || h.value === '')) {\n
                    displays[i].classList.add('is-invalid');\n
                    if (!bad) bad = displays[i];\n
                }\n
            }\n
            if (bad) {\n
                try { bad.focus(); } catch (e) {}\n
                return;\n
            }\n
            form.submit();\n
        } else if (el && el.form) {\n
            el.form.submit();\n
        }\n
    };\n
    document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {\n
        var disp = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-tdate-display]') : null;\n
        if (!disp) return;\n
        var order = orderOf(disp);\n
        // Filter only: never regroup mid-string edits (that scrambled\n
        // day/month changes into the year) and never move the caret\n
        // except to account for stripped illegal characters.\n
        var raw = disp.value || '';\n
        var cleaned = stripToDateChars(raw, fullLen(order));\n
        if (cleaned !== raw) {\n
            var caret = caretAfterStrip(raw, disp.selectionStart || 0, cleaned.length);\n
            disp.value = cleaned;\n
            try { disp.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (err) {}\n
        }\n
        var hidden = document.getElementById(disp.getAttribute('data-tdate-display'));\n
        var iso = cleaned.trim() === '' ? '' : parseToIso(cleaned, order);\n
        setHiddenIso(hidden, iso || '');\n
        disp.classList.toggle('is-invalid', cleaned.trim() !== '' && !iso);\n
    });\n
    // Blur: normalize a valid entry to tenant format (1/9/2026 ΓåÆ 01/09/2026,\n
    // 2026-09-11 ΓåÆ 11/09/2026, 11092026 ΓåÆ 11/09/2026); flag invalid ones.\n
    document.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) {\n
        var disp = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-tdate-display]') : null;\n
        if (!disp) return;\n
        var order = orderOf(disp);\n
        var hidden = document.getElementById(disp.getAttribute('data-tdate-display'));\n
        var t = (disp.value || '').trim();\n
        if (t === '') {\n
            setHiddenIso(hidden, '');\n
            disp.classList.remove('is-invalid');\n
            return;\n
        }\n
        var iso = parseToIso(t, order);\n
        if (iso) {\n
            setHiddenIso(hidden, iso);\n
            disp.value = toDisplay(iso, order);\n
            disp.classList.remove('is-invalid');\n
        } else {\n
            setHiddenIso(hidden, '');\n
            disp.classList.add('is-invalid');\n
        }\n
    });\n
    document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {\n
        var form = e.target;\n
        if (!form || !form.querySelector) return;\n
        form.querySelectorAll('[data-tdate-display]').forEach(function (disp) {\n
            var hidden = document.getElementById(disp.getAttribute('data-tdate-display'));\n
            if (hidden && disp.value && disp.value.length === fullLen(orderOf(disp))) {\n
                var iso = parseToIso(disp.value, orderOf(disp));\n
                if (iso) hidden.value = iso;\n
            }\n
        });\n
    }, true);\n
\n
    // Livewire tenant dates: [data-live-date-display] text <-> hidden wire:model.live ISO.\n
    // Typing a complete valid date pushes ISO to Livewire; server re-renders\n
    // re-sync the text (skipped while focused so partial typing is never wiped).\n
    function syncLiveWrap(wrap) {\n
        var hidden = wrap.querySelector('[data-live-date-hidden]');\n
        var disp = wrap.querySelector('[data-live-date-display]');\n
        if (!hidden || !disp) return;\n
        if (document.activeElement === disp) return;\n
        disp.value = toDisplay(hidden.value || '', orderOf(disp));\n
        disp.classList.remove('is-invalid');\n
    }\n
    function syncAllLiveDates() {\n
        document.querySelectorAll('[data-live-date-wrap]').forEach(syncLiveWrap);\n
    }\n
    document.addEventListener('input', function (e) {\n
        var disp = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-live-date-display]') : null;\n
        if (!disp || !disp.closest('[data-live-date-wrap]')) return;\n
        var wrap = disp.closest('[data-live-date-wrap]');\n
        var hidden = wrap.querySelector('[data-live-date-hidden]');\n
        if (!hidden) return;\n
        var order = orderOf(disp);\n
        // Filter only (same fix as the standard handler above): no regrouping,\n
        // caret preserved; tolerant parse pushes ISO to Livewire when valid.\n
        var raw = disp.value || '';\n
        var cleaned = stripToDateChars(raw, fullLen(order));\n
        if (cleaned !== raw) {\n
            var caret = caretAfterStrip(raw, disp.selectionStart || 0, cleaned.length);\n
            disp.value = cleaned;\n
            try { disp.setSelectionRange(caret, caret); } catch (err) {}\n
        }\n
        if (cleaned === '') {\n
            disp.classList.remove('is-invalid');\n
            if (hidden.value !== '') {\n
                hidden.value = '';\n
                hidden.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));\n
            }\n
            return;\n
        }\n
        var iso = parseToIso(cleaned, order);\n
        disp.classList.toggle('is-invalid', !iso);\n
        if (iso !== '' && hidden.value !== iso) {\n
            hidden.value = iso;\n
            hidden.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));\n
        }\n
    });\n
    document.addEventListener('livewire:updated', syncAllLiveDates);\n
    document.addEventListener('livewire:morph', syncAllLiveDates);\n
    document.addEventListener('livewire:init', function () {\n
        try {\n
            if (window.Livewire && window.Livewire.hook) {\n
                window.Livewire.hook('morph.updating', function (info) {\n
                    var el = info && info.el;\n
                    if (el && el.hasAttribute && el.hasAttribute('data-live-date-display') && document.activeElement === el) return false;\n
                });\n
                window.Livewire.hook('morph.updated', function () { syncAllLiveDates(); });\n
            }\n
        } catch (err) {}\n
    });\n
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', syncAllLiveDates);\n
\n
    // Calendar popup shared by [data-tdate-picker] and [data-live-date-picker].\n
    // Picked ISO dates fill the text input in tenant format (and notify\n
    // listeners / Livewire through the hidden ISO input).\n
    // Dynamic placement: below the button when it fits, above when the\n
    // field sits at the bottom of a card/modal, centered on small screens\n
    // or when neither side fits. Rendered on <body> so card/modal overflow\n
    // can never clip it. Repositioned on scroll/resize while open.\n
    var activePicker = null;\n
    function positionDatePicker() {\n
        var p = activePicker;\n
        if (!p || !p.popup || !p.btn) return;\n
        var r = p.btn.getBoundingClientRect();\n
        var pw = p.popup.offsetWidth || 292;\n
        var ph = p.popup.offsetHeight || 330;\n
        var vw = window.innerWidth || 360;\n
        var vh = window.innerHeight || 600;\n
        var margin = 8;\n
        var left, top;\n
        if (vw < 480) {\n
            left = Math.max(margin, (vw - pw) / 2);\n
        } else {\n
            left = r.right - pw;\n
            if (left < margin) left = margin;\n
            if (left + pw > vw - margin) left = vw - pw - margin;\n
        }\n
        if (vh - r.bottom >= ph + margin) {\n
            top = r.bottom + 4;\n
        } else if (r.top >= ph + margin) {\n
            top = r.top - ph - 4;\n
        } else {\n
            top = Math.max(margin, (vh - ph) / 2);\n
            left = Math.max(margin, (vw - pw) / 2);\n
        }\n
        p.popup.style.left = left + 'px';\n
        p.popup.style.top = top + 'px';\n
    }\n
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () { if (activePicker) positionDatePicker(); });\n
    document.addEventListener('scroll', function () { if (activePicker) positionDatePicker(); }, true);\n
    var CAL_MONTHS = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];\n
    function pad2(n) { return String(n).padStart(2, '0'); }\n
    function isoOfDate(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad2(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad2(d.getDate()); }\n
    function parseIso(iso) {\n
        var m = /^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/.exec(iso || '');\n
        if (!m) return null;\n
        var d = new Date(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3]);\n
        if (d.getFullYear() !== +m[1] || d.getMonth() !== +m[2] - 1 || d.getDate() !== +m[3]) return null;\n
        return d;\n
    }\n
    function closeDatePicker() {\n
        if (activePicker && activePicker.popup && activePicker.popup.parentNode) {\n
            activePicker.popup.parentNode.removeChild(activePicker.popup);\n
        }\n
        activePicker = null;\n
    }\n
    function pickerSelectedIso(p) {\n
        if (p.kind === 'live') {\n
            var lh = p.wrap ? p.wrap.querySelector('[data-live-date-hidden]') : null;\n
            return (lh && lh.value) || '';\n
        }\n
        return (p.hidden && p.hidden.value) || '';\n
    }\n
    function renderDatePicker(p) {\n
        var first = new Date(p.y, p.m, 1);\n
        var startDay = first.getDay();\n
        var daysIn = new Date(p.y, p.m + 1, 0).getDate();\n
        var prevDays = new Date(p.y, p.m, 0).getDate();\n
        var sel = pickerSelectedIso(p);\n
        var nowY = new Date().getFullYear();\n
        var minY = nowY - 120;\n
        var maxY = nowY + 10;\n
        if (p.maxIso) {\n
            maxY = Math.min(maxY, parseInt(p.maxIso.slice(0, 4), 10) || maxY);\n
            if (p.y > maxY) { p.y = maxY; p.m = 11; }\n
        }\n
        // Days past the ceiling render disabled (unpickable).\n
        var html = '<div class="tdate-cal-head">'\n
            + '<button type="button" data-cal="prev" aria-label="Previous month">&lsaquo;</button>'\n
            + '<select data-cal-month aria-label="Month">';\n
        for (var mi = 0; mi < 12; mi++) {\n
            html += '<option value="' + mi + '"' + (mi === p.m ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + CAL_MONTHS[mi] + '</option>';\n
        }\n
        html += '</select><select data-cal-year aria-label="Year">';\n
        for (var yy = maxY; yy >= minY; yy--) {\n
            html += '<option value="' + yy + '"' + (yy === p.y ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + yy + '</option>';\n
        }\n
        html += '</select>'\n
            + '<button type="button" data-cal="next" aria-label="Next month">&rsaquo;</button>'\n
            + '<button type="button" data-cal="today">Today</button>'\n
            + '</div><div class="tdate-cal-grid">';\n
        var DOW = ['Su', 'Mo', 'Tu', 'We', 'Th', 'Fr', 'Sa'];\n
        for (var i = 0; i < 7; i++) html += '<span class="dow">' + DOW[i] + '</span>';\n
        var k, d, iso, cls, dis;\n
        for (k = startDay - 1; k >= 0; k--) {\n
            d = new Date(p.y, p.m - 1, prevDays - k);\n
            iso = isoOfDate(d);\n
            dis = p.maxIso && iso > p.maxIso;\n
            cls = 'day muted' + (iso === sel ? ' sel' : '') + (dis ? ' disabled' : '');\n
            html += '<button type="button" class="' + cls + '" data-day="' + iso + '"' + (dis ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + d.getDate() + '</button>';\n
        }\n
        for (var day = 1; day <= daysIn; day++) {\n
            d = new Date(p.y, p.m, day);\n
            iso = isoOfDate(d);\n
            dis = p.maxIso && iso > p.maxIso;\n
            cls = 'day' + (iso === sel ? ' sel' : '') + (dis ? ' disabled' : '');\n
            html += '<button type="button" class="' + cls + '" data-day="' + iso + '"' + (dis ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + day + '</button>';\n
        }\n
        var tail = (7 - ((startDay + daysIn) % 7)) % 7;\n
        for (var t = 1; t <= tail; t++) {\n
            d = new Date(p.y, p.m + 1, t);\n
            iso = isoOfDate(d);\n
            dis = p.maxIso && iso > p.maxIso;\n
            cls = 'day muted' + (iso === sel ? ' sel' : '') + (dis ? ' disabled' : '');\n
            html += '<button type="button" class="' + cls + '" data-day="' + iso + '"' + (dis ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + t + '</button>';\n
        }\n
        p.popup.innerHTML = html + '</div>';\n
    }\n
    function pickIsoDate(p, iso) {\n
        if (p.kind === 'live') {\n
            var wrap = p.wrap;\n
            var lh = wrap ? wrap.querySelector('[data-live-date-hidden]') : null;\n
            var ld = wrap ? wrap.querySelector('[data-live-date-display]') : null;\n
            if (lh && (lh.value || '') !== iso) {\n
                lh.value = iso;\n
                try { lh.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); } catch (e) {}\n
            }\n
            if (ld) {\n
                ld.value = toDisplay(iso, orderOf(ld));\n
                ld.classList.remove('is-invalid');\n
            }\n
        } else {\n
            setHiddenIso(p.hidden, iso);\n
            if (p.disp) {\n
                p.disp.value = toDisplay(iso, orderOf(p.disp));\n
                p.disp.classList.remove('is-invalid');\n
                // Programmatic sets fire no events on their own: notify\n
                // filter forms (onchange="guardTdateSubmit(this)") so a\n
                // calendar-picked date actually refreshes the list. Booking\n
                // forms carry no change handler, so they are unaffected.\n
                try { p.disp.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true })); } catch (e) {}\n
            }\n
        }\n
        closeDatePicker();\n
    }\n
    function openDatePicker(btn, kind) {\n
        if (activePicker && activePicker.btn === btn) { closeDatePicker(); return; }\n
        closeDatePicker();\n
        var p = { btn: btn, kind: kind, popup: document.createElement('div'), y: 0, m: 0 };\n
        p.popup.className = 'tdate-cal';\n
        p.popup.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');\n
        if (kind === 'live') {\n
            p.wrap = btn.closest('[data-live-date-wrap]');\n
            var curLh = p.wrap ? p.wrap.querySelector('[data-live-date-hidden]') : null;\n
            var cur = parseIso(curLh ? curLh.value : '') || new Date();\n
            p.y = cur.getFullYear();\n
            p.m = cur.getMonth();\n
        } else {\n
            var id = btn.getAttribute('data-tdate-picker');\n
            p.hidden = document.getElementById(id);\n
            p.disp = document.getElementById(id + '_display');\n
            // Optional per-field ceiling (e.g. DOB never in the future):\n
            // data-tdate-max="Y-m-d" on the display or hidden input.\n
            var maxAttr = (p.disp && p.disp.getAttribute('data-tdate-max')) || (p.hidden && p.hidden.getAttribute('data-tdate-max')) || '';\n
            p.maxIso = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(maxAttr) ? maxAttr : '';\n
            var curT = parseIso(p.hidden ? p.hidden.value : '') || new Date();\n
            p.y = curT.getFullYear();\n
            p.m = curT.getMonth();\n
        }\n
        document.body.appendChild(p.popup);\n
        activePicker = p;\n
        renderDatePicker(p);\n
        positionDatePicker();\n
    }\n
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\n
        if ((e.key === 'Escape' || e.key === 'Esc') && activePicker) closeDatePicker();\n
    });\n
    // Month / year jump selects inside the popup (DOB-friendly: no endless stepping).\n
    document.addEventListener('change', function (e) {\n
        if (!activePicker) return;\n
        var sel = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-cal-month],[data-cal-year]') : null;\n
        if (!sel || !activePicker.popup.contains(sel)) return;\n
        if (sel.hasAttribute('data-cal-month')) {\n
            var mm = parseInt(sel.value, 10);\n
            if (!isNaN(mm) && mm >= 0 && mm <= 11) activePicker.m = mm;\n
        } else {\n
            var yy = parseInt(sel.value, 10);\n
            if (!isNaN(yy)) activePicker.y = yy;\n
        }\n
        renderDatePicker(activePicker);\n
    });\n
    document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        var el = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target : null;\n
        var pickerBtn = el && el.closest ? el.closest('[data-tdate-picker],[data-live-date-picker]') : null;\n
        if (pickerBtn) {\n
            if (pickerBtn.hasAttribute('data-tdate-picker')) openDatePicker(pickerBtn, 'tdate');\n
            else openDatePicker(pickerBtn, 'live');\n
            return;\n
        }\n
        if (!activePicker) return;\n
        // Clicks inside the popup (month/year selects, padding) keep it open.\n
        var inner = el && el.closest ? el.closest('.tdate-cal [data-day],.tdate-cal [data-cal]') : null;\n
        if (inner) {\n
            if (inner.hasAttribute('data-day')) { pickIsoDate(activePicker, inner.getAttribute('data-day')); return; }\n
            var nav = inner.getAttribute('data-cal');\n
            if (nav === 'prev' || nav === 'next') {\n
                var nm = activePicker.m + (nav === 'next' ? 1 : -1);\n
                var ny = activePicker.y;\n
                if (nm < 0) { nm = 11; ny--; }\n
                if (nm > 11) { nm = 0; ny++; }\n
                activePicker.m = nm;\n
                activePicker.y = ny;\n
                renderDatePicker(activePicker);\n
                positionDatePicker();\n
                return;\n
            }\n
            if (nav === 'today') {\n
                var now = new Date();\n
                pickIsoDate(activePicker, isoOfDate(now));\n
                return;\n
            }\n
            return;\n
        }\n
        if (el && el.closest && el.closest('.tdate-cal')) return;\n
        closeDatePicker();\n
    });\n
})();\n
</script>\n

// Global UID copy helper ΓÇö also in resources/js/app.js (inline fallback for non-Vite pages)\n
if (typeof window.copyToClipboard !== 'function') {\n
window.copyToClipboard = function(text, button) {\n
    if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {\n
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function(){\n
            var original = button.innerHTML;\n
            button.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-check-lg text-success"></i>';\n
            setTimeout(function(){ button.innerHTML = original; }, 2000);\n
        }).catch(function(){ fallbackCopy(text, button); });\n
    } else { fallbackCopy(text, button); }\n
    function fallbackCopy(val, btn){\n
        var input=document.createElement('input'); input.value=val; input.style.position='fixed'; input.style.opacity='0';\n
        document.body.appendChild(input); input.select(); try{ document.execCommand('copy'); }catch(e){} document.body.removeChild(input);\n
        var original=btn.innerHTML; btn.innerHTML='<i class="bi bi-check-lg text-success"></i>'; setTimeout(function(){ btn.innerHTML=original; },2000);\n
    }\n
};\n
}\n
